In addition, we introduce a temporal-aware semantic control (TASC) module that dynamically schedules semantic objectives according to the stage-wise characteristics of the denoising process, further improving semantic alignment and temporal consistency. Experiments show that KGEdit outperforms existing methods in editing precision and temporal stability, while offering higher efficiency and controllability in text-driven interaction scenarios.

</details>


### [39] [GiPL: Generative augmented iterative Pseudo-Labeling for Cross-Domain Few-Shot Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29539)
*Jiacong Liu,Shu Luo,Yikai Qin,Yaze Zhao,Yongwei Jiang,Yixiong Zou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GiPL是一种高效的双分支训练框架，通过迭代伪标签自训练和生成式数据增强，解决了跨域小样本目标检测中支持集利用不足和过拟合问题，在三个数据集上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言基础模型在跨域小样本目标检测中微调时面临两个关键挑战：支持集利用不足（稀疏的单实例标注）和严重过拟合（目标域样本极度有限）。

Method: 提出GiPL双分支训练框架：第一分支设计迭代伪标签自训练范式，在支持集上进行零样本推理生成可靠伪标注，与真实标签融合后迭代优化模型；第二分支引入基于大视觉-语言模型的生成式数据增强流程，合成域对齐的多目标标注图像以丰富训练样本。

Result: 在RUOD、CARPK、CarDD三个跨域小样本目标检测数据集上，1/5/10样本设置下，GiPL一致性地优于最先进方法，性能提升显著。

Conclusion: GiPL通过双分支策略有效提升了跨域小样本目标检测的性能，代码已开源。

Abstract: Vision-language foundation models have shown promising zero-shot generalization for Cross-Domain Few-Shot Object Detection (CD-FSOD). However, they face two critical challenges in fine-tuning: insufficient support set utilization due to sparse single-instance annotations, and severe overfitting under extremely limited target-domain samples. To address these issues, this paper proposes GiPL, an efficient two-branch training framework.In the first branch, we design an iterative pseudo-label self-training paradigm, which performs zero-shot inference on the support set to generate reliable pseudo-annotations, fuses them with ground-truth labels, and iteratively optimizes the model to fully exploit support set data. In the second branch, we introduce generative data augmentation pipeline using large vision-language models, which synthesizes domain-aligned, multi-object annotated images to enrich training samples and suppress overfitting. Extensive experiments on three challenging CD-FSOD datasets (RUOD, CARPK, CarDD) under 1/5/10-shot settings demonstrate that GiPL consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods with significant performance gains.Code is available at \href{https://github.com/z-yaz/CDiscover}{CDiscover}.

</details>


### [40] [Learning Representations from 3D Gaussian Splats](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29549)
*Julia Farganus,Krzysztof Żurawicki,Arkadiusz Gaweł,Weronika Jakubowska,Halina Kwaśnicka*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文对3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）场景表示下的几何深度学习架构进行了比较评估，研究了点云和图表模型在场景分类任务中的表现，并通过端到端分类、线性探测和聚类分析评估了潜在表示的质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS主要用于视图合成，其在场景理解任务中的潜力尚未被充分探索，因此需要系统性评估不同几何深度学习架构在3DGS表示上的分类能力。

Method: 采用点云和图表两种几何深度学习架构，在传统点云数据集和专用3DGS数据集上进行基准测试；将场景嵌入为潜在表示，并通过端到端分类、线性探测和聚类分析三种方式评估表示质量。

Result: 不同架构家族在性能上存在一致差异，高斯专属属性对表示质量有显著影响。

Conclusion: 研究为选择适合3DGS场景表示的几何感知架构和输入特征配置提供了指导，揭示了不同架构的适用性差异及高斯属性对表示学习的重要性。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) is a recent approach for scene rendering. Although primarily designed for view synthesis, its potential for scene understanding tasks remains underexplored. In this work, we conduct a comparative evaluation of various geometric deep learning architectures for the classification of 3D scenes represented using Gaussian Splatting. We benchmark point-based and graph-based models across both traditional point cloud datasets and dedicated Gaussian Splatting datasets. Scenes are embedded into latent representations, which are evaluated through end-to-end classification, linear probing, and clustering analysis. Our study provides insight into the suitability of different geometry-aware architectures and input feature configurations for learning effective 3D Gaussian Splat representations. The results highlight consistent differences between architectural families and reveal the impact of Gaussian-specific attributes on the quality of representation.

</details>


### [41] [TAE: Target-aware enhancer for nighttime UAV tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29558)
*Yanyan Chen,Ruigang Fu,Yu Song,Ping Zhong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对夜间低光照条件下无人机单目标跟踪中图像严重退化的问题，本文提出了一个目标感知的低光照增强框架TAE，通过跟踪边界框的弱监督信号进行区域感知增强，并采用自适应RGB多曲线融合机制实现不同区域的精细建模与自适应调整。同时贡献了包含268个序列、9个目标类别的夜间无人机跟踪基准DarkSOT，实验表明TAE在低光照夜间场景中显著提升了跟踪性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 夜间低光照条件下的图像严重退化是阻碍无人机单目标跟踪全天候应用的核心瓶颈，现有图像增强方法难以区分目标与背景区域，容易放大背景噪声或损害目标特征。

Method: 提出TAE框架：1）利用跟踪边界框的弱监督信号进行区域感知增强，使操作聚焦于目标区域；2）采用自适应RGB多曲线融合机制，实现不同区域的精细建模和自适应调整。

Result: 在DarkSOT和UAVDark135数据集上，TAE在低光照夜间场景中显著提升了跟踪性能，表现出强鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: TAE能够有效应对夜间低光照条件下的无人机目标跟踪挑战，区域感知增强和自适应RGB多曲线融合机制是提升性能的关键。DarkSOT数据集为相关研究提供了新的基准平台。

Abstract: Severe image degradation under low-light nighttime conditions constitutes a core bottleneck preventing all-day applications for UAV-based single object tracking. Existing image enhancement methods often struggle to distinguish between target and background regions, which can easily lead to amplified background noise or compromise target features. To overcome this limitation, we propose TAE, a target-aware low-light enhancement framework tailored for nighttime object tracking. Guided explicitly by weak supervisory signals from tracking bounding boxes, the framework performs region-aware enhancement to ensure operations focus on the target area. It further adopts an adaptive RGB multi-curve fusion mechanism to achieve refined modeling and adaptive adjustment across different regions. To facilitate research in this domain, we also contribute DarkSOT, a new benchmark for nighttime UAV tracking, comprising 268 sequences across 9 target categories. Experimental results on the DarkSOT and UAVDark135 demonstrate that TAE significantly improves tracking performance in low-light nighttime scenarios, exhibiting strong robustness and generalization. The DarkSOT dataset is available at https://github.com/Fu0511/DarkSOT-Dataset.

</details>


### [42] [From General Vision to Reliable Traversability Estimation: Adapting Vision Foundation Models for Unstructured Outdoor Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29565)
*Ji-Hoon Hwang,Jisung Bae,Dong-Wook Kim,Yeonkyu Lee,Seung-Woo Seo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ViTA框架通过可学习遍历性提示、视角多样化训练和几何知识蒸馏，将视觉基础模型（SAM2）适配为可靠的遍历性估计方法，在跨域泛化和误报抑制方面达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于视觉的遍历性估计方法依赖语义分割监督的视觉基础模型，但面临三个根本挑战：VFM的任务无关设计、遍历性标注的模糊性、以及语义标签与物理安全性之间的不一致。这些问题严重损害了遍历性估计的可靠性。

Method: 提出Vision-to-Traversability Adaptation (ViTA)框架，基于SAM2实现。具体包括：1) 通过可学习遍历性提示注入任务特定知识，保持VFM的跨域泛化能力；2) 视角多样化训练估计语义不确定性，抑制模糊边界处的自信预测；3) 蒸馏几何知识，使模型在推理时仅从RGB图像推断坡度和高程信息；4) 融合语义和几何输出为连续遍历性分数，反映语义不确定性和几何风险。

Result: 在多个领域（包括真实世界越野数据集）上的评估表明，ViTA在IoU和精确度上达到了SOTA，显著降低了误报率，并展现出强大的跨域泛化能力。

Conclusion: ViTA通过任务特定适配、不确定性建模和几何知识融合，有效解决了VFM应用于遍历性估计时的三大根本挑战，为非结构化环境下的可靠遍历性估计提供了新范式。

Abstract: Vision-based approaches have become the dominant paradigm for traversability estimation in unstructured outdoor environments, typically adapting vision foundation models (VFMs) via semantic segmentation supervision. However, this paradigm faces three fundamental challenges that undermine its reliability: the task-agnostic design of VFMs, the ambiguity of traversability annotations, and the discrepancy between semantic labels and physical safety. We propose Vision-to-Traversability Adaptation (ViTA), a framework that adapts VFMs for reliable traversability estimation, instantiated on SAM2. ViTA injects task-specific knowledge through learnable traversability prompts while preserving the VFM's cross-domain generalization. To handle annotation ambiguity, we introduce Perspective-Diversified Training, which estimates semantic uncertainty to suppress confident predictions at ambiguous boundaries. To bridge the semantic-traversability discrepancy, we distill geometric knowledge during training, enabling slope and elevation reasoning from RGB images alone at inference. The semantic and geometric outputs are fused into a continuous traversability score that reflects both semantic uncertainty and geometric risk. Evaluations across diverse domains, including challenging real-world off-road datasets, demonstrate that ViTA achieves state-of-the-art IoU and Precision with substantial false-positive reduction and strong cross-domain generalization.

</details>


### [43] [Optimizing Latent Representations for Robust Building Damage Assessment Onboard Earth Observation Satellites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29575)
*Thomas Goudemant,Benjamin Francesconi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于AI的星上建筑物损伤评估系统，通过在卫星上直接处理灾前和灾后高分辨率光学影像，实现目标级别的损伤定位与分类，减少数据下行链路延迟，提升应急响应效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然灾害或战区中快速识别受损建筑物对应急响应和优先级干预至关重要，但传统星载数据处理受限于数据下行、地面处理及人工判读的延迟，亟需降低这一延迟以提升决策响应速度。

Method: 将灾前影像在地面编码为紧凑的潜在表示并上传至卫星，与卫星新获取的灾后影像进行在轨对比；利用AI解释能力和星上处理能力，直接在数据源端进行处理，减少下行数据量同时保留任务相关信息；设计了孪生处理、交叉注意力、潜在空间压缩和鲁棒性数据增强等星上兼容变体并通过系统基准测试进行探索。

Result: 在xBD数据集上，该方法在存在配准误差的情况下仍表现出可靠且鲁棒的损伤评估性能，且在强压缩条件下性能退化极小。

Conclusion: 所提出的星上AI系统能够有效实现建筑物损伤的快速在轨评估，显著提升从数据采集到信息提取的整体系统响应性，为灾害应急响应提供更及时的支持。

Abstract: Rapid identification of damaged buildings after natural disasters or on war areas is crucial to support emergency response and prioritize interventions. Earth Observation constellations provide timely, large-scale coverage, but actionable information is often delayed by data downlink constraints, on-ground processing, and human interpretation. Reducing this latency is essential to improve decision-making responsiveness. In this work, we propose an original AI-based system that enables object-level building damage assessment (localization and damage classification) directly onboard satellites from pre-disaster and post-disaster highresolution optical imagery. Available pre-disaster images are encoded on ground into compact latent representations, transmitted to the satellite, and compared on-board with newly acquired post-event observations. Leveraging AI interpretation capabilities and increasing processing capabilities on-board satellites, the proposed design enables processing directly at the data source, reducing the amount of information to be downlinked while preserving task-relevant content and improving overall system responsivity. We explore the design space through a systematic benchmark of onboard-compatible variants, analyzing the impact of siamese processing, cross-attention, latent-space compression, and robustness-oriented data augmentation. Experiments on xBD dataset demonstrate reliable and robust damage assessment under misalignment, with minimal performance degradation under strong compression.

</details>


### [44] [Mitigating State Aliasing in Vision-Language-Action Models via Inverse Dynamics Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29577)
*Kyujin Lee,Injae Kim,Jihwan Park,Yejun Ju,Minseok Joo,Hyunwoo J. Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对VLA模型视觉特征中的状态混叠问题（视觉相似但动作不同），本文提出引入逆动力学学习作为辅助目标来直接监督视觉编码器，通过预测当前与未来观测间的动作，使编码器捕捉细粒度视觉差异，并利用伪反向监督提升泛化能力。该方法无需额外标注，在多个基准上取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型因使用预训练VLM的特征，对细粒度视觉差异不敏感，导致状态混叠（visually similar states that require substantially different actions）问题，影响底层控制精度。现有方法通过生成视觉/推理输出间接改善视觉理解，但未显式分析视觉特征空间中的状态混叠问题。

Method: 提出逆动力学学习作为辅助目标：通过预测当前观测与未来观测之间的动作，直接监督VLA视觉编码器。同时引入伪反向监督（pseudo-reversed supervision），将观测对反向以扩展动作方向多样性。该方法仅使用标准观测-动作对，无需额外标注，测试时保持原始推理流程不变。

Result: 在CALVIN ABC-D和SimplerEnv上，多个VLA基线均取得一致性能提升。通过冻结编码器探针实验和状态-特征对齐分析，证实该方法学习到状态判别性更强的视觉表示，有效减少了状态混叠，并更好地对齐了机器人状态变化。

Conclusion: 逆动力学学习作为一种轻量级辅助目标，能有效缓解VLA模型中的状态混叠问题，提升视觉特征对细粒度动作差异的区分能力，且具有通用性和零推理开销的优势。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have emerged as a promising framework that unifies perception, reasoning, and control for robot manipulation by adapting pretrained vision-language models (VLMs) to action prediction. However, VLM-derived representations are often insensitive to subtle visual distinctions required for low-level control, causing state aliasing between visually similar states that require substantially different actions. Prior VLA studies improve visual understanding by generating visual or reasoning outputs, such as future frames, 2D grounding points or traces, or intermediate spatial reasoning steps, but these objectives typically shape the vision encoder only indirectly through end-to-end prediction and do not explicitly analyze state aliasing in the learned visual feature space. To mitigate state aliasing, we introduce inverse dynamics learning as an auxiliary objective that directly supervises the VLA vision encoder. By predicting the action between current and future observations, our objective encourages the encoder to capture fine-grained visual distinctions that determine low-level actions. We further use pseudo-reversed supervision to expose the encoder to a broader range of action directions and improve generalization under limited robot demonstrations. Our method applies to diverse VLA baselines, uses only standard observation-action pairs without additional annotations, and preserves the original inference pipeline at test time. Experiments on CALVIN ABC-D and SimplerEnv show consistent gains across diverse VLA baselines. Frozen-encoder probing and state-feature alignment analyses further show that our method learns state-discriminative visual representations that reduce state aliasing and better align with robot state changes.

</details>


### [45] [ReactBench: A Cause-Driven Benchmark for Multimodal Hallucination via Systematic Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29579)
*Shizhe Zhou,Bohan Jia,Kai Wu,Yan Shen,Tongyun Li,Yuyang Wu,Shaohui Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ReactBench是一个因果驱动的大模型幻觉基准测试，通过四种对抗性任务和考试式评估格式，系统性地暴露多模态大语言模型在不同幻觉诱因下的脆弱性，并利用思维链推理定位细粒度子原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有幻觉基准测试主要关注检测幻觉结果而非评估其根本原因，且多依赖简单场景和有限评估格式，无法挑战最新模型。作者旨在构建一个系统化、可解释的测试平台，从因果层面诊断多模态模型的幻觉问题。

Method: 通过生成对抗性图像和幻觉诱导查询，设计了四项针对性任务：关系擦除（暴露共现偏差）、反事实属性（暴露语言先验）、变化追踪（暴露跨图像比较缺陷）、密集计数（暴露细粒度感知瓶颈）。采用考试式评估格式，并利用思维链推理在每个任务中识别细粒度的子原因。

Result: 广泛评估表明，当前MLLMs在各类因果特异性幻觉触发下仍然显著脆弱，验证了ReactBench作为系统化、可解释测试平台的有效性。

Conclusion: ReactBench为诊断和提升多模态模型鲁棒性提供了系统化且可解释的测试平台，填补了现有基准在因果层面评估幻觉的空白。

Abstract: While multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have achieved rapid progress in vision-language understanding, they remain prone to multimodal hallucinations, producing responses that are inconsistent with the visual input. Existing benchmarks predominantly focus on detecting hallucination outcomes rather than evaluating the underlying causes of these failures. Moreover, many benchmarks rely on simplistic scenarios and limited evaluation formats that no longer challenge state-of-the-art models. To address these limitations, we introduce ReactBench, a cause-driven hallucination benchmark featuring multiple tasks and an exam-style evaluation format. By generating adversarial images and hallucination-inducing queries, ReactBench introduces four targeted tasks: Relational Erasure, Counterfactual Attribute, Alteration Tracing, and Dense Counting. These tasks systematically expose co-occurrence bias, language priors, cross-image comparative perception deficiencies, and fine-grained perceptual bottlenecks. Beyond standard accuracy-based evaluation, we leverage Chain-of-Thought reasoning to identify fine-grained sub-causes of hallucination within each task. Extensive evaluations reveal that current MLLMs remain notably vulnerable to cause-specific hallucination triggers, demonstrating the value of ReactBench as a systematic and interpretable testbed for diagnosing and improving multimodal model robustness. The project page is available at https://reactbench.github.io/.

</details>


### [46] [BitC-3DGS: High-Capacity 3D Gaussian Splatting Watermarking via Bit Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29583)
*Yuquan Bi,Baosheng Yu,Yingke Lei,Jianwei Yang,Hongsong Wang,Jie Gui,Yuan Yan Tang,James Tin-Yau Kwok*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BitC-3DGS提出了一种针对3D高斯泼溅(3DGS)资产的高容量水印框架，通过位压缩令牌化方案将多个消息比特编码为单个语义令牌，突破CLIP文本编码器77个令牌的长度限制，支持128比特消息容量且恢复精度与现有方法64比特相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于预训练文本编码器的比特到令牌水印方法受CLIP固定77个令牌上下文长度限制，无法嵌入超过77比特的丰富信息（如所有权、来源和认证码），难以满足大规模3D资产管线中对高容量水印的需求。

Method: 提出位压缩令牌化方案，将同一块内的多个消息比特编码为单个语义令牌；设计双分支架构实现联合块解压和比特解码；引入硬消息采样策略增强解码器训练时的组合覆盖范围。

Result: 在Blender和LLFF数据集上，BitC-3DGS实现128比特消息容量，恢复精度与现有最先进方法64比特消息相当，同时保持渲染保真度。

Conclusion: BitC-3DGS有效突破了CLIP文本编码器的令牌长度限制，实现了3DGS资产的高容量水印嵌入与高精度恢复，为大规模3D资产管线的可靠标识和完整性验证提供了可行方案。

Abstract: High-capacity watermarking is necessary for 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) assets to embed rich information (e.g., ownership, provenance, and authentication codes), enabling reliable identification and integrity verification in large-scale 3D asset pipelines. Existing bit-to-token watermarking methods based on a pre-trained text encoder are limited to 77-bit messages due to CLIP's fixed 77-token context length, as tokens beyond this limit are unsupported by learned positional embeddings. To address this limitation, we introduce BitC-3DGS, a bit-compression framework that encodes multiple message bits per token. It employs a bit-compressed tokenization scheme that encodes multiple bits within the same chunk into a single semantic token. To enable recovery of the compressed information, it further introduces a dual-branch architecture for joint chunk decompression and bit decoding, along with a hard-message sampling strategy to improve combinatorial coverage during decoder training. Extensive experiments on the Blender and LLFF datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of BitC-3DGS for high-capacity watermarking, achieving high message recovery accuracy and rendering fidelity. For example, it supports 128-bit message capacity with recovery accuracy comparable to that of 64-bit messages in recent state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [47] [Brain-IT-VQA: From Brain Signals to Answers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29588)
*Roman Beliy,Matias Cosarinsky,Oliver Heinimann,Navve Wasserman,Michal Irani*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Brain-IT-VQA 框架，通过脑信号解码语言 token 并结合语言模型实现视觉问答，大幅优于此前方法；同时构建 NSD-VQA 数据集（每图20个QA对，覆盖20个受控问题类别），为 fMRI 视觉问答提供更可靠、可解释的基准，并用于分析不同脑区对各类问题的贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 fMRI 视觉问答方法性能有限，且缺乏能够可靠、可解释地评估脑解码能力的基准数据集；同时，已有模型很少被用于理解大脑视觉表征结构。因此，本文旨在提出更强的预测框架和更细粒度的评测基准，以推动该领域发展并揭示大脑视觉信息表征机制。

Method: 基于 Brain Interaction Transformer (Brain-IT)，从 fMRI 信号中解码语言 token，并将其与语言模型整合以回答视觉问题。同时构建 NSD-VQA 数据集，每张自然图像平均提供20个问答对，覆盖20个受控问题类别，从多个层次解耦视觉理解任务。

Result: Brain-IT-VQA 在多个指标上显著优于此前基于 fMRI 的图像描述和 VQA 方法；NSD-VQA 提供了更可靠和可解释的评估；利用该基准量化了可从 fMRI 信号中可靠解码的视觉与语义信息类型，并分析了不同脑区在不同问题类别下的贡献差异。

Conclusion: Brain-IT-VQA 与 NSD-VQA 共同构成了一个兼具强预测能力和脑表征研究功能的框架，为理解大脑如何编码多层次的视觉语义信息提供了新的工具和基准。

Abstract: Decoding visual content from fMRI signals recorded while a person views images, and specifically answering questions about the seen images, is a long-standing challenge. While significant progress has been made in recent years in visual question answering (VQA) from fMRI, performance remains limited. Moreover, although recent models can make increasingly accurate predictions, they have rarely been used as tools for understanding the structure of visual representations in the brain. We present Brain-IT-VQA, a framework for visual question answering from fMRI. Building on the Brain Interaction Transformer (Brain-IT), our method decodes language tokens from brain activity and integrates them with a language model to answer visual questions. Our model substantially outperforms previous fMRI-based captioning and VQA approaches. We further introduce NSD-VQA, a new dataset and benchmark for visual question answering from fMRI. Unlike existing image-fMRI VQA datasets, which typically provide only a few broad and weakly controlled questions per image, NSD-VQA provides on average 20 question-answer pairs per image across 20 controlled question categories that disentangle multiple levels of visual understanding. This enables more reliable and interpretable evaluation despite limited fMRI test data. Together, Brain-IT-VQA and NSD-VQA provide both a strong predictive framework and a tool for studying brain representations. Using this benchmark, we quantify which forms of visual and semantic information can be reliably decoded from fMRI responses to natural images. We further analyze the contributions of different brain regions across question types.

</details>


### [48] [Non-Forgetting Knowledge Allocation with Bi-level Competition for Class-Incremental Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29592)
*Xiang Tan,Run He,Yawen Cui,Mengchen Zhao,Yan Wu,Tianyi Chen,Huiping Zhuang,Xiaonan Luo,Guanbin Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对基于预训练模型（PTMs）的类增量学习（CIL）中，现有适配器方法存在知识分配忽略任务差异且易遗忘的问题，提出了一种无遗忘分配与双层竞争机制（NoFA-BC）。该方法通过递归最小二乘构建无遗忘分配器（NFA），并引入任务内胜者全拿（WTA）和任务间最后淘汰（LOF）的双层竞争（BLC），实现适配器知识自适应的按需分配，同时加入稳定性增强（SE）提升旧任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于适配器的方法在推理时为每个适配器分配统一的知识分配，忽略了任务差异的本质，导致适配器利用次优；此外，在类增量学习约束下，分配器在任务演化时容易发生遗忘。

Method: 提出NoFA-BC方法，包含三个组件：1）无遗忘分配器（NFA）：将分配器训练转化为递归最小二乘问题，实现等价于全数据训练的分配器；2）双层竞争（BLC）：任务内层采用胜者全拿（WTA）机制提取任务内最显著logit表示适配器贡献，任务间层采用最后淘汰（LOF）机制抑制不相关适配器；3）稳定性增强（SE）：进一步提升旧任务性能。

Result: 通过BLC机制，每个适配器的参与比例可以根据输入进行定制化调整；无遗忘分配器避免了增量学习中的遗忘问题；稳定性增强进一步提升了旧任务的表现。

Conclusion: NoFA-BC通过无遗忘分配器和双层竞争机制，有效解决了基于适配器的类增量学习中知识分配不均和遗忘问题，实现了适配器知识的自适应分配。

Abstract: Class-Incremental Learning (CIL) with pre-trained models (PTMs) aims to sequentially adapt PTMs to new categories without forgetting old knowledge. Built upon PTMs, existing adapter-based methods mainly train models via distinct task-specific adapters, and present a uniform knowledge allocation for each adapter during inference. However, this allocation mechanism ignores the nature of task discrepancy and leads to suboptimal utilization of adapters. Also, under CIL constraint, an allocator is prone to forgetting when tasks evolve. To address these issues, we propose a Non-Forgetting Allocation with Bi-Level Competition (NoFA-BC). NoFA-BC constructs a non-forgetting allocator (NFA) by transforming the allocator training into a recursive least-squares problem and achieves an allocator equivalent to that trained with all data. Based on the NFA, a Bi-Level Competition (BLC) including an intra-task level Winner-Takes-All (WTA) mechanism and inter-task Last-Ones-Fall (LOF) elimination is proposed to provide better allocation of adapter knowledge. WTA extracts the most significant logit within a task to represent the adapter's contribution and LOF suppresses the irrelevant adapters. With BLC, participation ratio of each adapter can be tailored for each input. Moreover, a Stability Enhancement (SE) process is incorporated to further improve the performance of old tasks.

</details>


### [49] [CogniVerse: Revolutionizing Multi-Modal Retrieval-Augmented Generation with Cognitive Reflection and Geometric Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29602)
*Xiang Fang,Wanlong Fang,Changshuo Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CogniVerse 是一个受认知启发的多模态检索增强生成框架，通过认知反思模块、基于黎曼流形和对齐的多模态检索模块以及基于最优传输的分层生成模块，解决了现有 MMRAG 中的噪声检索、跨模态语义错位、缺乏自适应推理等问题，在准确性和连贯性上显著超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 MMRAG 框架存在检索噪声大、跨模态语义不对齐、缺乏自适应推理以及局部与全局上下文生成不连贯等关键限制，亟需一种更鲁棒且具有认知启发性的解决方案。

Method: CogniVerse 包含三个协同组件：(1) 认知反思模块——动态评估检索必要性并过滤相关多模态内容；(2) 多模态检索模块——利用信息几何在黎曼流形中对齐嵌入，并通过谱图理论精化知识图谱；(3) 分层生成模块——采用基于最优传输的损失函数平衡 token 级准确性与全局语义连贯性。

Result: 在准确性和连贯性上显著优于现有最先进系统，同时降低了检索延迟。

Conclusion: CogniVerse 通过认知启发的数学方法有效解决了 MMRAG 的多项核心挑战，为多模态知识密集型问答提供了更高效、更准确的解决方案。

Abstract: Multi-modal Retrieval-Augmented Generation (MMRAG) has emerged as a powerful paradigm for enhancing Multimodal Large Language Models in knowledge-intensive question answering by integrating external visual, textual, and structural knowledge. However, existing MMRAG frameworks suffer from critical limitations, including noisy and irrelevant retrieval, cross-modal semantic misalignment, lack of adaptive reasoning, and incoherent generation across local and global contexts. We introduce \textbf{CogniVerse}, a novel MMRAG framework that addresses these challenges through a cognitive-inspired, mathematically rigorous approach. Drawing from human-like reasoning, CogniVerse integrates three synergistic components: (1) a Cognitive Reflection Module that dynamically assesses retrieval necessity and filters relevant multi-modal content, reducing noise and computational overhead; (2) a Multi-modal Retrieval Module that aligns embeddings in a Riemannian manifold using information geometry and refines knowledge graphs via spectral graph theory, ensuring precise and coherent retrieval; and (3) a Hierarchical Generation Module that employs an optimal transport-based loss to balance token-level accuracy and global semantic coherence. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CogniVerse significantly outperforms state-of-the-art systems in both accuracy and coherence, while reducing retrieval latency.

</details>


### [50] [Learning Context-Conditioned Predicate Semantics via Prototype Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29610)
*NamGyu Jung,Chang Choi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AlignG提出了一种通过原型反馈学习上下文条件谓词语义的方法，在场景图生成中动态调整谓词表示，在VG-150和GQA-200数据集上取得一致改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 场景图生成中多义谓词的语义随上下文变化，现有方法使用静态原型或检索相似样本无法根据图像特定证据重组语义，导致歧义上下文中的系统性混淆。

Method: AlignG从每张图像内的关系候选中推断上下文条件谓词语义，并将自适应后的语义反馈回关系表示进行重新校准；学习目标将这些自适应锚定到全局语义中心，在防止语义漂移的同时允许场景提供一致关系线索时的选择性重组。

Result: 在VG-150和GQA-200上SGDet任务中F@100分别提升+1.4和+2.7；可视化显示原型根据场景证据选择性地合并或分离谓词。

Conclusion: 通过原型反馈实现上下文条件的动态谓词语义适应，有效解决了场景图生成中谓词多义性问题，超越现有方法。

Abstract: In scene graph generation, a central challenge is modeling polysemous predicates whose meanings shift across contexts. Prior approaches address this issue by decomposing predicates into multiple static prototypes or retrieving semantically similar exemplars. However, these strategies keep predicate representations static and cannot reorganize semantics to reflect image-specific evidence, leading to systematic confusions in ambiguous contexts. We propose AlignG, which learns context-conditioned predicate semantics via prototype feedback. AlignG infers context-conditioned predicate semantics from the relation candidates within each image and feeds the adapted semantics back to recalibrate relation representations. The learning objective anchors this adaptation to global semantic centers, preventing semantic drift while still allowing selective reorganization when the scene provides consistent relational cues. Experiments on VG-150 and GQA-200 show consistent improvements over state-of-the-art baselines, with F@100 improvements of +1.4 on VG-150 and +2.7 on GQA-200 under SGDet. We further visualize per-image prototype similarity shifts and observe coherent context-dependent reorganization where prototypes selectively merge or separate predicates according to scene evidence. The code is available at https://github.com/Namgyu97/AlignG-SGG.pytorch.

</details>


### [51] [AgentCVR: Active Multi-Agent Cross-Video Reasoning via Script-Simulated Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29643)
*Yilun Qiu,Jiahe Wang,Cilin Yan,Jiayin Cai,Xiaolong Jiang,Yao Hu,Chun Yuan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AgentCVR，一种用于跨视频推理的多智能体框架，通过主智能体协调视觉和音频智能体进行证据提取，并引入脚本模拟强化学习优化策略，在CVR基准上超越单次基线方法，性能媲美闭源系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大语言模型在处理跨视频推理时，采用单次编码策略将多个视频压缩到共享上下文中，容易模糊稀有关键证据，导致CVR任务表现不佳。

Method: 提出AgentCVR多智能体框架：主智能体迭代协调专门的视觉和音频智能体进行目标性证据提取；并引入脚本模拟强化学习，利用LLM生成的语义脚本和轻量文本模拟器优化策略，避免在线探索的高成本多模态推理。

Result: 在综合CVR基准上，AgentCVR超越单次基线方法，性能与最先进闭源系统相当，尤其在复杂跨视频对齐和定位任务上表现突出。

Conclusion: AgentCVR通过将CVR建模为主动证据获取任务并采用多智能体协作框架，有效解决了跨视频推理中的关键证据丢失问题，代码已开源。

Abstract: Cross-Video Reasoning (CVR) has emerged as a critical frontier in multimodal intelligence, requiring models to retrieve, align, and aggregate evidence distributed across multiple videos. Current Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) often struggle with CVR, as simple single-pass strategies encode multiple videos into a shared compressed context, potentially obscuring rare but critical evidence. In this paper, we propose AgentCVR, a multi-agent framework that treats CVR as an active evidence-acquisition task. AgentCVR employs a Master Agent to iteratively coordinate specialized Visual and Audio Agents for targeted evidence extraction. To ensure efficient training, we introduce Script-Simulated RL, which optimizes the agent's policy with LLM-generated semantic scripts and a lightweight text-based simulator, bypassing costly multimodal inference during online exploration. Experimental results on a comprehensive CVR benchmark show that AgentCVR outperforms single-pass baselines and achieves comparable performance to state-of-the-art closed-source systems, particularly in complex cross-video alignment and localization. To ensure reproducibility, our code is available at https://github.com/wang-jh24/AgentCVR.

</details>


### [52] [MARTIAN: A Rendering Framework for Aerial Mars Imagery from HiRISE Orbital Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29647)
*Dario Pisanti,Georgios Georgakis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MARTIAN是一个基于Blender的开源渲染框架，利用真实HiRISE轨道地图产品生成火星地表逼真航拍视图，支持可控光照和不同高度，为视觉导航提供精确位姿标注。该框架已在Ingenuity等火星旋翼机地图匹配定位系统中得到验证，合成训练的图像匹配器成功在真实火星图像上评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 火星表面航拍视觉导航需要适应多样化光照条件和地形形态的视觉管线，但大规模带标注的火星航拍数据集极度匮乏，成为训练和评估此类方法的瓶颈。

Method: 基于Blender的开源渲染框架，利用真实HiRISE轨道地图产品，在可控光照条件和不同高度下合成火星地形的逼真航拍视图，并生成精确的位姿标注。

Result: 该框架已部署应用于Ingenuity及未来火星旋翼机的地图匹配定位系统，合成训练的深度图像匹配器成功在真实火星图像上完成评估。

Conclusion: MARTIAN有效解决了火星航拍视觉导航训练数据稀缺的问题，为火星旋翼机视觉导航提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Aerial navigation on Mars requires vision-based pipelines that are robust to the diverse illumination conditions and terrain morphology of the Martian surface. A key bottleneck for training and evaluating such methods is the scarcity of large-scale, annotated aerial datasets. We present MARTIAN, an open-source Blender-based rendering framework that leverages real HiRISE orbital map products to synthesize realistic aerial views of the Martian terrain under controllable lighting conditions and at varying altitudes. MARTIAN generates observations with accurate pose annotations, directly addressing the scarcity of training data for vision-based navigation on Mars. The framework has been validated through its deployment in concurrent work on map-based localization systems for Ingenuity and future Mars rotorcraft, where synthetically trained deep image matchers were successfully evaluated on real Mars imagery. MARTIAN is publicly available at: https://github.com/nasa-jpl/martian.

</details>


### [53] [OccamToken: Efficient VLM Inference with Training-Free and Budget-Adaptive Token Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29657)
*Geng Li,Guohao Chen,Ting Chen,Shilin Shan,Kuangji Zuo,Bofan Lyu,Tuo An,Gen Li,Jianfei Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OccamToken提出了一种无需训练的视觉token压缩框架，通过register锚定的相对证据测试替代传统的绝对排名剪枝范式，在保持93%以上准确率的同时将2880个视觉token压缩至约40个。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉token剪枝方法采用绝对排名范式（固定Top-K选择），存在三个根本缺陷：注意力沉溺（attention sinks）扭曲token重要性排序、图像冗余性导致固定budget不可靠、查询依赖的视觉证据需要动态调整。这些缺陷使得固定token预算在不同输入间表现不稳定。

Method: 提出OccamToken框架，核心创新是用register锚定的相对证据测试替代绝对token排名。利用register token天然吸收低信息注意力模式的特性，将其作为稳定参考基准，通过动态阈值分别实现图像自适应冗余剪枝和查询自适应相关性剪枝。

Result: 在LLaVA-NeXT、LLaVA-v1.5和Qwen3-VL上一致提升精度-效率权衡。在LLaVA-NeXT上，将2880个视觉token压缩至约40个（保留1.4%），仍保持93%以上原始准确率。

Conclusion: 证明了register锚定的相对证据测试范式在视觉token压缩中的有效性，实现了极端压缩率下的稳定性能，无需额外训练即可推广到多个VLM架构。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) rely on long visual token sequences for visual understanding, making the prefill stage expensive in both computation and memory. Most existing pruning methods follow an absolute-ranking paradigm, assigning importance scores to visual tokens and retaining a fixed top-K subset. In this work, we argue that this paradigm is fundamentally brittle: attention sinks distort token importance rankings, while image redundancy and query-dependent visual evidence make fixed token budgets unreliable across inputs. We propose OccamToken, a training-free framework that replaces absolute token ranking with register-anchored relative evidence testing. Instead of asking which tokens are globally important, OccamToken evaluates whether a visual token provides information beyond a register-based reference. Our key insight is that register tokens naturally absorb low-information attention patterns, making them a stable reference for identifying genuinely informative visual evidence. Based on this principle, OccamToken performs both image-adaptive redundancy pruning and query-adaptive relevance pruning through dynamic thresholds derived from register attention. Across LLaVA-NeXT, LLaVA-v1.5, and Qwen3-VL, OccamToken consistently improves the accuracy-efficiency trade-off without additional training. Notably, on LLaVA-NeXT, it reduces 2,880 visual tokens to approximately 40 while preserving over 93% of the original accuracy, enabling stable visual token compression even in the extreme 1.4% retention regime.

</details>


### [54] [Geometry-Guided Modeling of Foundation Features Enables Generalizable Object Shape Deformation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29661)
*Yiyao Ma,Kai Chen,Zhongxiang Zhou,Zhuheng Song,Dongsheng Xie,Zelong Tan,Rong Xiong,Qi Dou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为GODeform的通用变形学习框架，通过将类别级形状模板变形匹配目标观测来恢复单目3D形状，引入几何引导的特征建模和视角自适应特征聚合模块，在跨视角、跨类别泛化上显著超越SOTA方法，并支持灵巧机器人操作任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单目3D形状恢复在任意视角和未见物体类别上实现鲁棒泛化仍面临重大挑战，现有方法在处理大形状变化和多样视角时表现有限。

Method: 1) 几何引导的特征建模：用模板拓扑增强基础特征得到几何感知表示，再与目标观测显式关联以指导变形；2) 视角自适应特征聚合：利用多视角模板特征及对应相机位姿丰富规范模板表示，实现与任意目标视角的鲁棒特征对齐。

Result: 在应对大形状变化和多样视角上显著优于SOTA方法，展现出对未见类别的强泛化能力，并有效支持真实世界灵巧机器人操作任务。

Conclusion: 本文提出的GODeform框架通过显式模板变形和创新的特征建模机制，有效解决了单目3D形状恢复中的泛化难题，并验证了其在机器人操作等下游任务中的应用价值。

Abstract: Monocular 3D shape recovery is fundamental to geometric understanding, yet achieving robust generalization across arbitrary viewpoints and unseen object categories remains a significant challenge. In this paper, we present a generalizable deformation learning framework that reconstructs 3D objects by explicitly deforming a category-level shape template to match the target observation. To address complex shape variations between the template and the target, we introduce a geometry-guided feature modeling mechanism. This process first enriches foundation features with template topology to yield a geometry-aware representation, which is then explicitly correlated with the target observation to guide precise deformation. Furthermore, to bridge the disparity between the fixed template and arbitrary target views, we propose a view-adaptive feature aggregation module. This module leverages multi-view template features and their corresponding camera poses to enrich the canonical template representation, ensuring robust feature alignment regardless of the target's perspective. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our approach significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods in handling large shape variations and diverse viewpoints, exhibiting strong generalization to novel categories and effectively supporting downstream real-world dexterous robotic manipulation tasks. Project homepage: https://GODeform.github.io/

</details>


### [55] [SAFE-Pruner: Semantic Attention-Guided Future-Aware Token Pruning for Efficient Vision-Language-Action Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29662)
*Shilin Ma,Chubin Zhang,Changyuan Wang,Yuji Wang,Yue Wu,Zixuan Wang,Jingqi Tian,Zheng Zhu,Yansong Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SAFE-Pruner，一种利用未来层注意力线索进行视觉token剪枝的即插即用框架，在VLA模型推理中实现最高1.89倍加速且成功率下降不到1.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉token剪枝方法主要依赖浅层线索做剪枝决策，容易丢弃深层所需的关键视觉信息，导致加速不稳定。

Method: 1) 发现语义注意力一致性现象，即VLA模型在各执行步骤中将注意力概率集中在同一语义实体上；2) 基于此设计前向展望策略预测深层token显著性，防止过早移除关键token；3) 引入自适应子任务划分策略检测注意力突移，提升预测精度和剪枝可靠性。

Result: 仿真和真实实验中实现最高1.89倍加速，成功率下降低于1.7%，比SOTA方法提升最高1.9%。

Conclusion: SAFE-Pruner通过引入未来层注意力线索有效解决了现有剪枝方法中关键token过早丢弃的问题，实现了稳定且高效的VLA模型推理加速。

Abstract: Real-time inference of vision-language-action (VLA) models is essential for robotic control. While visual token pruning has shown strong potential for accelerating inference, most existing methods mainly base pruning decisions on shallow-layer cues and risk discarding visual information required by deep layers. To address this issue, we propose SAFE-Pruner, a plug-and-play pruning framework that incorporates attention cues of future layers into pruning decisions. Specifically, we identify semantic attention consistency, the tendency that VLA models concentrate their attention probability mass on the same semantic entity across execution steps. Based on this observation, we design a forward-looking strategy to forecast the token saliency in deep layers, which prevents the premature removal of critical tokens and leads to more stable acceleration. We further introduce an adaptive subtask division strategy to detect abrupt attention shifts, thereby improving forecasting accuracy and pruning reliability. Extensive experiments in simulation and real-world settings demonstrate that our method achieves up to 1.89x speedup with a minimal degradation in success rate of less than 1.7%, while outperforming state-of-the-art methods by up to 1.9%.

</details>


### [56] [Unsupervised Semantic Segmentation Facilitates Model Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29691)
*Xiaoyan Yu,Lisa Mais,Jannik Franzen,Peter Hirsch,Nick Lechtenbörger,Andreas Mardt,Dagmar Kainmüller*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种简单直观的可视化协议，基于无监督语义分割结果来理解自监督视觉Transformer（ViT）的行为，无需追求分割性能最优，而是揭示模型在不同层和表示中一致出现的特性，如位置偏置、尺度行为以及边界伪影等，有助于区分位置效应与局部性偏置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有研究揭示了对比学习（CL）与掩码图像建模（MIM）训练出的ViT在自注意力机制和表示信息上存在显著差异，但这些发现尚未充分普及到更广泛的社区，导致CL模型的见解常被错误地推广到MIM模型。为了简化模型理解过程，使非专业用户也能直观理解不同SSL模型的行为差异，作者提出了一个简单易解释的可视化协议。

Method: 基于无监督语义分割的可视化协议：对不同SSL模型（包括CL和MIM）在各层和不同表示上进行基准测试，通过可视化分割结果来观察模型的行为模式，而非追求分割性能最大化。

Result: 获得了关于位置偏置（positional biases）和尺度行为（scaling behaviors）的新见解，包括在DINOv3-Large模型令牌中发现了强烈的边界伪影（boundary artifacts）。这些发现补充并有助于传达此前一系列研究成果。

Conclusion: 该可视化协议能够清晰区分位置效应与密切相关的局部性偏置（locality bias），后者在文献中已被广泛研究。协议已公开于GitHub，有望推动更广泛的社区对自监督ViT模型进行更深入的模型理解。

Abstract: Self-supervised learning (SSL) has produced a diverse landscape of vision transformers (ViTs) whose pretrained representations support a wide range of downstream tasks. Towards a better understanding of these models, a body of work has assessed the mechanics of their self-attention as well as the types of information captured across their representations, revealing, for example, stark differences between models trained with contrastive learning (CL) and masked image modeling (MIM). However, these advances in model understanding have not yet fully permeated the broader community, where insights specific to CL models are sometimes generalized to MIM models. To make model understanding straightforward and intuitive for a broad audience, we propose a simple and easily interpretable visualization protocol.
  Our protocol is based on visualizing unsupervised semantic segmentation results, yet our goal is not to maximize segmentation performance. Instead, it allows us to convey model behaviors that consistently emerge across images. Benchmarking a diverse set of SSL models across layers and representations, we obtain novel insights into distinct positional biases and scaling behaviors, including strong boundary artifacts in DINOv3-Large model tokens. These insights complement and help communicate a range of previous findings.
  Our protocol further enables a clear visual distinction between positional effects and the closely related but distinct locality bias, which has been studied much more extensively in the literature. The protocol is publicly available on GitHub and we believe it will catalyze further model understanding for a broad community.

</details>


### [57] [SLAD : Shared LoRA Adapters for Task Specific Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29726)
*Reda Bensaid,Yassir Bendou,Vincent Gripon,François Leduc-Primeau*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SLAD方法，通过低秩适应和参数共享策略改善师生模型间的特征对齐，在资源受限场景下实现更高效的任务特定知识蒸馏，比微调快2倍且性能更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在资源受限环境（如嵌入式系统）中，使用缩小版基础模型进行下游任务适配日益流行。先前工作发现教师模型微调虽提升性能，但线性探测反而更利于知识蒸馏。本文旨在探究这一现象的原因并解决教师微调导致师生特征表示不对齐的问题。

Method: (1) 利用低秩适应（LoRA）替代全参数微调，减少师生特征表示偏移；(2) 提出参数共享策略，在联合训练中让教师和学生的适配器共享参数，进一步增强特征对齐。整体方法称为SLAD。

Result: SLAD在多个分类和分割数据集上达到任务特定蒸馏框架的最优性能；不仅提升了学生模型性能，也提升了教师模型性能；训练速度比微调快2倍。

Conclusion: 教师微调导致特征不对齐是知识蒸馏效果下降的主要原因；通过LoRA和参数共享实现特征对齐，可以同时提升师生双方性能并加速训练；SLAD为资源受限环境下的模型适配提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: In the context of resource-constrained environments such as embedded systems, adapting reduced-size foundation models to downstream tasks has become increasingly popular. This has recently motivated the emerging setting of task-specific distillation, where a larger and a smaller version of the same foundation model are both adapted to the same downstream task, with the goal of transferring knowledge from the former to the latter. Recent work has demonstrated the benefits of using a larger version of the same foundation model to assist the adaptation of a smaller one. Typically, the larger model (teacher) is first adapted via fine-tuning or linear probing before its knowledge is distilled into the smaller model (student). While fine-tuning the teacher often increases its performance, recent work showed that probing it leads to better knowledge distillation to the student. Our findings show that this is mainly due to a mis-alignment in feature representation between the teacher and the student which occurs during the teacher's fine-tuning. Inspired by existing efforts to preserve previously learned knowledge, we first propose leveraging low-rank adaptation, resulting in better feature alignment and therefore better knowledge transfer. Drawing from this insight, we further enhance the feature alignment through a parameter-sharing strategy of the adapters between the two encoders during joint training. Our proposed method, SLAD, shows better feature alignment between the teacher and student, which results in increased performance for not only the student but also the teacher model, while being 2x faster to train than fine-tuning. Through extensive experiments on multiple classification and segmentation datasets, we demonstrate the improved accuracy and transfer efficiency of our method, achieving state-of-the-art performance in the task-specific distillation framework.

</details>


### [58] [Energy-Aware NECO for Single-Pass Pixel-wise Out-of-Distribution Detection in Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29773)
*Boyuan Zhang,Huanshan Huang,Yifei Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Energy-Aware NECO，一种单次前向传播的像素级分布外（OOD）检测器，通过融合NECO风格几何比率和基于logit的Energy分数，在语义分割中实现了比MC Dropout等基线更优的OOD检测性能（AUROC 0.8539），同时保持单次推理的高效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动机器人的可靠语义分割需要在分布偏移下同时具备准确密集预测和鲁棒不确定性估计；现有强基线如Monte Carlo Dropout需要多次随机前向传播，难以部署在边缘平台上。

Method: 将解码器特征上计算的中心化NECO风格几何比率与基于logit的Energy分数相结合，两种成分通过纯分布内验证集拟合的统计量进行标准化，并通过凸组合融合，形成单次前向传播的混合OOD检测分数。

Result: 在miniMUAD数据集的真实像素级OOD标签上，混合分数AUROC达0.8539，优于NECO-only（0.8280）、Energy-only（0.8171）以及集成预测熵基线（0.8124）。

Conclusion: 混合检测器在保持单次前向传播效率优势的同时，整体排序性能优于各单独成分和基线方法；代码已开源。

Abstract: Reliable semantic segmentation for mobile robots requires both accurate dense prediction and robust uncertainty estimation under distribution shift. Strong uncertainty baselines such as Monte Carlo Dropout often require repeated stochastic forward passes and are difficult to deploy on edge platforms.
  We propose Energy-Aware NECO, a single-pass pixel-wise out-of-distribution (OOD) detector for semantic segmentation. The method combines a centered NECO-style geometric ratio computed from decoder features with a logit-based Energy score. Both components are standardized using statistics fitted on a pure in-distribution validation split and fused through a convex combination.
  We evaluate the method on the miniMUAD subset using true pixel-level OOD labels. The proposed hybrid score achieves an AUROC of 0.8539, outperforming NECO-only (0.8280), Energy-only (0.8171), and an ensemble predictive-entropy baseline (0.8124). Additional qualitative and operating-point analyses show that the hybrid detector improves overall ranking performance while preserving the efficiency advantages of a single-pass design.
  Code is available at https://github.com/boyuan-zhangx/Energy-Aware_NECO

</details>


### [59] [Improving CLIP Adaptation by Breaking Tail Alignment for Source-Free Cross-Domain Few-Shot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29776)
*Shuai Yi,Yixiong Zou,Yuhua Li,Ruixuan Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对CLIP在跨域小样本学习中的过拟合问题，提出自适应尾头对齐（ATHA）策略，通过主动推远低相似度“尾令牌”与文本嵌入的对齐，同时加强高相似度“头令牌”的对齐，在四个基准上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CLIP在跨域场景下性能严重下降，传统方法对所有图像令牌与文本嵌入进行统一对齐，但研究发现强制对齐低相似度令牌会导致过拟合，需要一种自适应的对齐策略。

Method: 提出Adaptive Tail-Head Alignment (ATHA)，将传统统一对齐范式转变为自适应对齐范式：对高相似度“头令牌”加强对齐，对低相似度“尾令牌”削弱对齐（主动推远），避免过拟合于稀缺训练数据。

Result: 在四个具有挑战性的跨域小样本学习基准上取得最先进性能。

Conclusion: 在跨域和小样本条件下，强行对齐所有图像令牌并不合理；对语义不足的尾令牌打破对齐反而更有益，ATHA通过自适应调整对齐强度有效缓解了过拟合问题。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) such as CLIP demonstrate strong zero-shot generalization, but their performance significantly degrades in cross-domain scenarios with scarce target-domain training data (Cross-Domain Few-Shot Learning, CDFSL). In this paper, we focus on the target-domain few-shot finetuning in the CLIP-based CDFSL task. Prevailing finetuning paradigms uniformly align all image patch tokens with their corresponding textual embeddings. However, we find a counterintuitive phenomenon: actively pushing away certain low-similarity image tokens, termed "tail tokens", from their textual embeddings consistently improves target-domain performance. We delve into this phenomenon and provide a novel interpretation: under great domain shifts and scarce training data, the model can hardly extract semantic information from visual inputs; therefore, the common belief of alignment is valid only for tokens already containing sufficient semantic information; for tail tokens, forcing the alignment would lead to excessive overfitting to the scarce training, while breaking the alignment is more useful. Motivated by this, we propose Adaptive Tail-Head Alignment (ATHA), a novel fine-tuning strategy for CLIP that transforms the conventional uniform alignment paradigm to an adaptive alignment paradigm, with both alignment strengthening and weakening. Extensive experiments on four challenging CDFSL benchmarks validate our state-of-the-art performance. Our code is available at https://github.com/shuaiyi308/ATHA.

</details>


### [60] [Fewer Steps, Better Performance: Efficient Cross-Modal Clip Trimming for Video Moment Retrieval Using Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29793)
*Xiang Fang,Daizong Liu,Wanlong Fang,Pan Zhou,Zichuan Xu,Wenzheng Xu,Junyang Chen,Renfu Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SpotVMR，一种高效的即插即用模块，用于视频时刻检索任务。该方法通过学习识别与语言查询相关的视频区域，并引入低成本语义索引特征来捕捉上下文信息，同时利用蒸馏损失解决联合训练中的优化问题，在三个数据集上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VMR方法将未裁剪视频下采样为固定长度片段，导致：1) 长视频处理不可行；2) 查询相关帧可能被过滤掉，导致边界模糊，引入边界偏差和推理偏差。

Method: 提出SpotVMR方法：1) 设计片段搜索模型，学习根据语言查询识别有希望的视频区域进行搜索；2) 引入低成本语义索引特征，捕捉对象和交互的上下文信息以指导搜索；3) 使用蒸馏损失解决片段选择器与VMR模型端到端联合训练中的优化问题。

Result: 在三个具有挑战性的数据集上进行了广泛实验，验证了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: SpotVMR作为一种高效的即插即用模块，能够提升现有VMR方法的效率，同时保持良好检索性能，有效解决了长视频处理中的边界偏差和推理偏差问题。

Abstract: Given an untrimmed video and a sentence query, video moment retrieval using language (VMR) aims to locate a target query-relevant moment. Since the untrimmed video is overlong, almost all existing VMR methods first sparsely down-sample each untrimmed video into multiple fixed-length video clips and then conduct multi-modal interactions with the query feature and expensive clip features for reasoning, which is infeasible for long real-world videos that span hours. Since the video is downsampled into fixed-length clips, some query-related frames may be filtered out, which will blur the specific boundary of the target moment, take the adjacent irrelevant frames as new boundaries, easily leading to cross-modal misalignment and introducing both boundary-bias and reasoning-bias. To this end, in this paper, we propose an efficient approach, SpotVMR, to trim the query-relevant clip. Besides, our proposed SpotVMR can serve as plug-and-play module, which achieves efficiency for state-of-the-art VMR methods while maintaining good retrieval performance. Especially, we first design a novel clip search model that learns to identify promising video regions to search conditioned on the language query. Then, we introduce a set of low-cost semantic indexing features to capture the context of objects and interactions that suggest where to search the query-relevant moment. Also, the distillation loss is utilized to address the optimization issues arising from end-to-end joint training of the clip selector and VMR model. Extensive experiments on three challenging datasets demonstrate its effectiveness.

</details>


### [61] [Low-Magnification SEM May Suffice: Interpretable Deep Learning for Multi-Scale Fracture-Cause Classification in Zirconia-Toughened Alumina](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29798)
*Julian Schmid,Pawel Astankow,Tom Vater,Julius Beck,Robert Cichon,Danny Krautz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可解释的视觉Transformer（ViT）工作流，用于自动分类氧化铝基复合材料（BIOLOX delta）髋膝关节植入物的断裂原因。基于8493张SEM图像（50x-10000x）的数据库，模型在严重类别不平衡下达到0.907准确率和0.888宏F1分数，且低倍率（50x）性能与高倍率相当，Grad-CAM归因一致定位到经典断口特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前陶瓷植入物断口分析工作流程耗时、部分主观且依赖高倍率扫描电镜（SEM），亟需自动化、可解释的方法来提高质量保证和患者安全的效率。

Method: 构建包含8493张SEM图像的数据集，标注为三种制造缺陷类别；采用微调视觉Transformer（ViT）进行分类，使用分层五折交叉验证评估性能；通过感知哈希/SSIM两阶段泄漏审计确保数据无重叠；利用Grad-CAM进行可解释性分析。

Result: 微调ViT达到0.907准确率和0.888宏F1分数；低倍率（50x）性能与高倍率（1k-10kx）相当；Grad-CAM定位到镜像区、人字纹、气孔、加工痕迹等经典断口特征；泄漏审计确认样本无重叠。

Conclusion: 可解释ViT可作为陶瓷植入物质量保证的补充工具，实现低倍率预筛查，减少对耗时高倍率检查的依赖。

Abstract: Reliable identification of fracture origins in alumina matrix composite hip and knee implants is critical for quality assurance and patient safety, yet current fractographic workflows are time-consuming, partly subjective, and reliant on high-magnification scanning electron microscopy (SEM). We present an interpretable vision-transformer (ViT) workflow for automated classification of fracture causes in an alumina matrix composite (BIOLOX delta, CeramTec GmbH) widely used in total joint replacements. A dataset of 8,493 SEM images (50x-10,000x) was curated from five years of in-production burst and proof tests and annotated into three defect categories defined along the manufacturing chain: green body, hard machining, and material defects. Under severe class imbalance, the fine-tuned ViT reached an accuracy of 0.907 and a macro-F1 of 0.888 in stratified five-fold cross-validation, with a two-stage perceptual-hash/SSIM leakage audit confirming negligible specimen overlap. Notably, performance at low magnification (50x) was comparable to that at high magnification (1k-10kx), indicating that macro-scale features - mirror geometry and hackle line fields - already encode sufficient diagnostic signal. Grad-CAM attributions consistently localised on canonical fractographic cues (mirrors, hackles, pores, machining marks), aligning with established fractographic criteria. Together, these results position interpretable ViTs as a complementary tool for ceramic-implant quality assurance, enabling low-magnification pre-screening and reducing reliance on time-intensive high-magnification inspection.

</details>


### [62] [Not All Inputs Are Valid: Towards Open-Set Video Moment Retrieval Using Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29812)
*Xiang Fang,Wanlong Fang,Daizong Liu,Xiaoye Qu,Jianfeng Dong,Pan Zhou,Renfu Li,Zichuan Xu,Lixing Chen,Panpan Zheng,Yu Cheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次提出开放集视频时刻检索（OS-VMR）任务，并设计OpenVMR模型，利用归一化流区分ID和OOD查询，在拒绝OOD查询的同时检索ID查询对应的精确时刻。在三个数据集上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VMR方法基于封闭集假设，无法处理开放场景下的OOD查询，会导致错误检索，在高风险场景（如犯罪检测）中造成不可挽回的损失。因此需要探索OS-VMR这一新设定。

Method: 使用归一化流学习ID查询分布（假设服从多元高斯分布），引入不确定性分数搜索ID-OOD分离边界，拉近ID查询特征以细化边界。设计视频-查询匹配和帧-查询匹配分别实现粗粒度和细粒度跨模态交互，最后引入正-无标签学习模块进行时刻检索。

Result: 在三个VMR数据集上的实验验证了OpenVMR的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文首次探索了OS-VMR任务，提出的OpenVMR模型能够有效区分ID和OOD查询并完成检索，为开放集VMR研究提供了新方向。

Abstract: Video Moment Retrieval (VMR) targets to retrieve the specific moment corresponding to a sentence query from an untrimmed video. Although recent works have made remarkable progress in this task, they implicitly are rooted in the closed-set assumption that all the given queries as video-relevant\footnote{In this paper, we treat ``video-relevant query'' as ``in-distribution (ID) query'' and ``video-irrelevant query'' as ``out-of-distribution (OOD) query''.}. Given an OOD query in open-set scenarios, they still utilize it for wrong retrieval, which might lead to irrecoverable losses in high-risk scenarios, \textit{e.g.}, criminal activity detection. To this end, we creatively explore a brand-new VMR setting termed Open-Set Video Moment Retrieval (OS-VMR), where we should not only retrieve the precise moments based on ID query, but also reject OOD queries. In this paper, we make the first attempt to step toward OS-VMR and propose a novel model \textbf{OpenVMR}, which first distinguishes ID and OOD queries based on the normalizing flow technology, and then conducts moment retrieval based on ID queries. Specifically, we first learn the ID distribution by constructing a normalizing flow, and assume the ID query distribution obeys the multi-variate Gaussian distribution. Then, we introduce an uncertainty score to search the ID-OOD separating boundary. After that, we refine the ID-OOD boundary by pulling together ID query features. Besides, video-query matching and frame-query matching are designed for coarse-grained and fine-grained cross-modal interaction, respectively. Finally, a positive-unlabeled learning module is introduced for moment retrieval. Experimental results on three VMR datasets show the effectiveness of our OpenVMR.

</details>


### [63] [Fairness Beyond Demographics: Optimizing Performance Across Appearance-Based Hidden Cohorts in Medical Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29827)
*Milad Masroor,Cuong Nguyen,Kevin Wells,Gustavo Carneiro*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出无标签隐藏队列公平性（LHCF）训练范式，通过图像外观聚类发现潜在亚群并优化其公平性，无需人口统计标签即可在单/多人口属性上实现最先进公平性，避免了传统方法忽视潜在分层和数据稀疏性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学图像分析公平性方法仅针对可见的人口统计属性（如性别、年龄）单独优化，存在两大问题：1）忽略图像外观中更隐蔽的潜在分层，这些分层可能揭示模型错误的更深层来源；2）同时考虑多个人口属性时导致训练数据稀疏，难以扩展。

Method: 提出LHCF训练范式：将图像聚类为K个基于外观的隐藏队列，然后在这些队列上进行公平性优化，而非直接对可见人口属性进行优化。该方法无需使用人口统计标签进行训练。

Result: 在提出的HIDFairBench公平性基准上，LHCF在单个人口属性和多个人口属性上均实现了最先进的公平性结果。

Conclusion: 隐藏队列公平性是一种实用、可扩展且稳健的替代方案，可替代基于人口统计的公平性优化方法，用于可信的医学图像分析。

Abstract: Medical image analysis models can exhibit performance disparities across patient subgroups, threatening clinical safety and fairness. Existing methods typically address this issue by optimizing accuracy and fairness metrics for visible demographic attributes (e.g., sex or age) considered in isolation. This strategy not only overlooks potentially more informative latent stratifications, which may reveal deeper sources of model error and inequity, but also fails to scale when multiple demographic attributes are considered simultaneously due to the resulting sparsity of training data within each subgroup. We deal with these issues by introducing the label-free hidden-cohort fairness (LHCF) training paradigm that instead of maximizing fairness over visible demographic attributes, it optimizes fairness across latent subpopulations discovered from image appearance. By clustering images into K appearance-based cohorts and applying fairness optimization over them, LHCF uncovers underlying sources of model error and avoids the combinatorial sparsity of multi-demographic attributes, reducing disparities across both single and multiple demographic attributes. We demonstrate on our proposed fairness benchmark, HIDFairBench, that LHCF provides state-of-the-art fairness results on single and multiple demographic attributes, despite never using demographic labels for training. Our results position hidden-cohort fairness as a practical, scalable, and robust alternative to demographic-based fairness optimization for trustworthy medical image analysis.

</details>


### [64] [Parameter-Efficient Subspace Decoupling ViT for Mitigating Multi-Task Negative Transfer in Histological Scoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29852)
*Youhan Huang,Jiajun Li,Yilin Fang,Shuai Wang,Chuheng Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于子空间解耦的多任务视觉Transformer（ViT），通过轻量级任务特定Adapter和正交性约束，在非酒精性脂肪肝病（NAFLD）组织学评分中实现了脂肪变性、气球样变和炎症三个高度相关指标的独立特征子空间构建，有效减少任务间干扰，并在自建的小鼠NAFLD多任务数据集上验证了方法的有效性和计算效率优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NAFLD组织学评分自动化面临两大挑战：一是组织学标注成本高昂，二是NAS各指标（脂肪变性、气球样变、炎症）高度相关，在多任务学习中易产生负迁移，导致性能下降。现有方法难以在共享表示与任务特异性之间取得平衡。

Method: 提出子空间解耦的多任务Vision Transformer（ViT），在Transformer中集成轻量级任务特定Adapter模块，并施加正交性约束，为脂肪变性、气球样变和炎症分别构建独立的特征子空间，保留共享表示的同时减少任务间干扰。

Result: 在自建的多任务小鼠NAFLD组织学数据集上，该方法相比分别训练单任务模型，在显著降低计算成本的同时，提升了多任务学习的稳定性和泛化性能。

Conclusion: 子空间解耦策略能有效缓解高度相关任务间的负迁移问题，为NAFLD自动化评分提供了一种高效且可复现的多任务学习方案。代码和数据集将在论文接收后公开。

Abstract: Histological scoring is essential for diagnosing Non-Alcoholic Fatty Liver Disease (NAFLD), yet its automation remains challenging due to the high annotation cost and negative transfer among the strongly correlated NAFLD Activity Score (NAS) indicators in multi-task learning. To address this issue, we propose a subspace-decoupled multi-task Vision Transformer (ViT) that integrates lightweight task-specific Adapters with orthogonality-based constraints. This design constructs independent feature subspaces for steatosis, ballooning, and inflammation, effectively reducing task interference while retaining shared representations. We further construct a curated multi-task mouse NAFLD histology dataset with expert annotations for all NAS components. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed method improves multi-task stability and generalization with substantially reduced computational cost compared to training separate single-task models. The code and the curated dataset have been prepared and will be made publicly available upon acceptance to support reproducibility.

</details>


### [65] [Masked Diffusion Vision-Language Models for Temporal Action Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29858)
*Fengshun Wang,Zhengbo Zhang,Zhigang Tu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MDVLM-TAL方法，将掩码扩散视觉语言模型适配于时间动作定位任务，通过可迭代编辑的语义和边界令牌、边界感知掩码及步级IoU奖励，解决了自回归解码器中后期语义无法修正早期预测以及标准扩散训练与TAL不匹配的问题，在多个数据集上取得优于自回归基线的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于视觉语言公式的TAL方法采用自回归解码器从左到右生成令牌，导致后期语义证据无法修正早期时间戳预测，限制了定位精度。

Method: 将掩码扩散视觉语言模型（MDVLM）适配于TAL，使语义令牌和边界令牌在迭代去噪过程中通过双向注意力保持可编辑状态；提出Planned Training Objective（边界感知掩码+步加权重建）和Step-Level IoU Reward以解决标准扩散训练与TAL任务的不匹配问题。

Result: 在ActivityNet-RTL、ActivityNet-1.3和THUMOS-14数据集上，MDVLM-TAL优于自回归视觉语言基线，在更严格的时间IoU标准下提升尤为显著。

Conclusion: 掩码扩散模型通过迭代联合优化语义和边界令牌，能有效改进TAL中的时间推理和边界定位能力。

Abstract: Temporal action localization (TAL) requires recognizing the target event and localizing its start and end times precisely in untrimmed videos. Recent vision-language formulations improve semantic reasoning and support language-conditioned outputs, but their autoregressive decoders still generate tokens from left to right, preventing later semantic evidence from revising earlier timestamp predictions.
  We adapt masked diffusion vision-language models (MDVLMs) to TAL so that semantic tokens and boundary tokens remain editable throughout iterative denoising with bidirectional attention, allowing temporal boundaries and semantic content to be refined jointly.
  Direct adaptation, however, creates two TAL-specific mismatches: standard masked diffusion training corrupts all positions uniformly at random, but the time tokens are more reliable when enough semantic context is available; and token-level cross-entropy does not reflect temporal IoU. To address these mismatches, we introduce a Planned Training Objective that uses boundary-aware masking and step-weighted reconstruction to rehearse the late recovery of time tokens, together with a Step-Level IoU Reward that provides overlap-aware supervision during denoising. A standard sequence-level cross-entropy term provides the base reconstruction signal.
  Experiments on ActivityNet-RTL, ActivityNet-1.3, and THUMOS-14 show that MDVLM-TAL improves both temporal reasoning and boundary localization over autoregressive vision-language baselines, with especially strong gains under stricter temporal IoU criteria.

</details>


### [66] [DGSG-Mind: Dynamic 3D Gaussian Scene Graphs for Long-Term Scene Understanding and Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29879)
*Luzhou Ge,Xiangyu Zhu,Jinyan Liu,Xuesong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DGSG-Mind 提出了一种混合实例感知的3D高斯动态场景图系统，结合概率体素网格与显式3D高斯实现鲁棒的跨模态实例融合和增量语义建图，并通过3D高斯Mind实现多模态推理，在零样本3DVG、语义分割和场景重建上取得最优性能，并部署到真实机器人上验证了其目标导向推理和动态更新能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态3D场景表示方法存在跨视角实例关联脆弱、难以处理物体级拓扑变化、缺乏显式空间推理能力以及依赖离线真实3D几何等问题。

Method: 提出DGSG-Mind系统，包含：(1) 概率体素网格与显式3D高斯耦合实现跨模态实例融合与增量语义建图；(2) 基于高斯的视觉重定位和局部掩码精化机制处理动态变化；(3) 构建层次化场景图并开发3D高斯Mind，融合结构关系、空间语义信息和RoI高斯渲染进行多模态推理。

Result: 在自建地图方法中取得最优的零样本3DVG性能，同时在3D开放词汇语义分割和场景重建上也表现强劲，并在真实机器人上验证了目标导向推理和动态更新能力。

Conclusion: DGSG-Mind通过混合实例感知的高斯动态场景图有效解决了动态场景下的跨模态实例融合、拓扑变化处理和空间推理问题，为长期具身场景理解提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Integrating open-vocabulary semantic information into dynamic 3D scene representations is essential for long-term embodied scene understanding. However, existing methods often suffer from fragile instance association due to incomplete cross-view cues, while their limited ability to handle object-level topological changes restricts long-term robotic task execution. Moreover, current 3D scene understanding methods either rely on simple feature matching without explicit spatial reasoning or assume offline ground-truth 3D geometry. To address these challenges, we present DGSG-Mind, a hybrid instance-aware 3D Gaussian dynamic scene graph system with an embodied reasoning agent. Our system couples a probabilistic voxel grid with explicit 3D Gaussians to enable robust cross-modal instance fusion and incremental semantic mapping. It handles dynamic changes through Gaussian-based visual relocalization and localized masked refinement guided by geometric-semantic consistency. Built on the instance Gaussian map, DGSG-Mind further constructs a hierarchical scene graph and develops the 3D Gaussian Mind, which integrates structural relations, spatial-semantic information, and visually annotated RoI Gaussian renderings for multimodal reasoning. Extensive experiments show that DGSG-Mind achieves the best zero-shot 3DVG performance among methods operating on self-reconstructed maps, while also delivering strong performance in 3D open-vocabulary semantic segmentation and scene reconstruction. We further deploy DGSG-Mind on real-world robots to demonstrate its target-oriented reasoning and dynamic update capabilities. The project page of DGSG-Mind is available at https://icr-lab.github.io/DGSG-Mind

</details>


### [67] [Train the Agent, Not the Expert: Learning to Harness Heterogeneous Experts for Multi-Turn Visual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29894)
*Yaowu Fan,Tao Han,Dazhao Du,Andy J. Ma,Jia Wan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VisHarness是一种可训练视觉智能体，通过解耦高层感知、推理、决策与底层任务执行，学习调用异构视觉专家模型来解决复杂视觉任务，在多个基准上优于通用模型并达到与专用模型竞争的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉模型通常针对孤立的单一任务优化，难以支持需要复杂语言理解和密集小目标感知的通用视觉智能。作者希望在不牺牲专用模型精度的前提下，实现智能体的通用视觉能力。

Method: VisHarness将高层感知、推理、决策与底层任务执行解耦，通过轻量级训练学习一个可泛化的视觉专家调用策略，利用多轮交互与动态视觉记忆归档机制缓解视觉token开销，实现高效的在线策略强化学习训练。

Result: 在推理分割、广义指代分割、密集小目标检测和指代计数四个基准上，VisHarness显著优于现有通用模型，并达到或超越专用模型的性能。

Conclusion: 通过智能体调度专家模型的范式，可以在保留通用智能的同时充分利用专用模型的精度优势，是一种有效的通用视觉智能实现路径。

Abstract: Recent progress in computer vision has produced a wide range of powerful specialized models for detection, segmentation, counting, and other visual tasks. However, these models are usually optimized for isolated task formulations, making it difficult to directly support general-purpose visual intelligence, especially when a task requires complex language understanding and dense small-object perception. In this paper, we propose VisHarness, a trainable visual agent that decouples high-level perception, reasoning, and decision-making from low-level task execution. Instead of training a model to solve a specific visual task, VisHarness learns to harness a set of carefully designed heterogeneous visual experts. This paradigm preserves the general intelligence of the agent while fully leveraging the precision advantages of specialized visual models in concrete visual tasks. With only lightweight training, VisHarness learns a generalizable visual expert-harnessing policy and can solve common fundamental vision tasks under various complex conditions through multi-turn interactions with visual expert models. To enable efficient on-policy reinforcement learning training in a live environment, we introduce dynamic visual memory archiving, which mitigates the rapidly accumulating visual-token overhead caused by multi-turn interactions with visual expert models. Experiments on four representative benchmarks covering reasoning segmentation, generalized referring segmentation, dense small-object detection, and referring counting demonstrate that VisHarness substantially outperforms existing general-purpose models and achieves competitive or superior performance compared with task-specific models.

</details>


### [68] [CityGen: Structure-Guided City-Style Synthesis for Cross-City Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29935)
*Zezhong Qian,Zhao Yang,Lu Tan,Zhihao Yan,Weiyi Hong,Haizhuang Liu,Yawei Jueluo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CityTransfer-Bench，一个用于评估自动驾驶系统跨城市泛化能力的地理分离基准，以及CityGen，一个基于扩散模型的零标签城市自适应生成框架，通过高清地图条件合成和城市级视觉提示引导，在感知、分割和规划等多个任务上显著提升跨城市鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶系统通常在有限地理区域内训练和评估，当部署到新城市时，因外观、道路拓扑和交通模式的显著域偏移导致性能严重下降。现有方法依赖目标域标签、城市特定标注或任务特定设计，可扩展性和全面评估能力受限。

Method: 提出CityTransfer-Bench作为地理分离的跨城市泛化评估基准，覆盖感知、分割和规划任务；同时提出CityGen，一个基于扩散模型的生成框架，通过高清地图条件合成和城市级视觉提示引导，实现零标签城市自适应。

Result: 大量实验表明，CityGen在多个任务上持续提升跨城市鲁棒性，建立了可扩展且标签高效的泛化基础。

Conclusion: CityGen为可泛化的自动驾驶提供了一种可扩展、标签高效的解决方案，有效解决了跨城市部署中的域偏移问题。

Abstract: Autonomous driving systems are commonly trained and evaluated within limited geographic regions, which hinders their scalability when deployed in new cities. However, significant domain shifts in appearance, road topology, and traffic patterns often cause severe performance degradation under cross-city deployment. Existing approaches based on domain adaptation, data augmentation, or synthetic data generation typically rely on labeled target data, city-specific annotations, or task-specific designs, limiting their scalability and effectiveness for holistic evaluation. In this paper, we introduce CityTransfer-Bench, a geographically disjoint benchmark for evaluating cross-city generalization across perception, segmentation, and planning, and propose CityGen, a diffusion-based generative framework that performs zero-label city adaptation via HD-map-conditioned synthesis guided by city-level visual prompts. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CityGen consistently improves cross-city robustness across multiple tasks, establishing a scalable and label-efficient foundation for generalizable autonomous driving.

</details>


### [69] [Mesh-Aware Epipolar Matching for Multi-View Multi-Person 3D Pose Estimation in Basketball](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29953)
*Li Yin,Qin Haobin,Tomohiro Suzuki,Calvin Yeung,Mariko Isogawa,Keisuke Fujii*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种无训练框架MAEM，通过单目3D人体网格恢复模型前端和两阶段极线匹配策略，在团队体育场景中实现多视角多人3D姿态估计，在两个篮球数据集上取得竞争性结果（MPJPE分别为59.8mm和74.0mm）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 团队体育场景中多人遮挡、队服外观相似以及标注数据稀缺，导致学习方法的泛化能力受限，而无训练方法的性能受限于2D关键点检测精度和跨视角关联鲁棒性。

Method: 提出Mesh-Aware Epipolar Matching (MAEM)无训练框架，前端使用单目3D人体网格恢复模型，后接两阶段极线匹配策略：结合基于并查集的聚类与逐关节三角测量，实现鲁棒的跨视角关联和精确的3D姿态重建。

Result: 在SportCenter EPFL上MPJPE/PA-MPJPE为59.8/40.7 mm，在Human-M3 Basketball上为74.0/51.8 mm，优于现有无训练关联基线方法，在室内外篮球场景中达到竞争性的纯RGB性能。

Conclusion: 稠密网格几何信息对跨视角关联是有效的，无需目标域训练或微调即可取得优异性能。

Abstract: Multi-view multi-person 3D pose estimation in team sports scenarios remains challenging due to player occlusions, appearance similarity caused by team uniforms, and the scarcity of annotated multi-view data, all of which limit the effectiveness and generalization capability of learning-based methods. In contrast, the performance of training-free approaches is inherently constrained by the accuracy of 2D keypoint detection and the robustness of cross-view association. To address these challenges, we propose Mesh-Aware Epipolar Matching (MAEM), a training-free framework for multi-view multi-person 3D pose estimation. Our method employs a monocular 3D human mesh recovery model as the frontend and introduces a two-stage epipolar matching strategy based on the recovered mesh outputs. Specifically, the proposed framework combines disjoint-set-union-based clustering with per-joint triangulation to achieve robust cross-view association and accurate 3D pose reconstruction. Experiments on two public multi-view basketball datasets demonstrate that MAEM consistently outperforms existing training-free association baselines while achieving competitive RGB-only performance in both indoor and outdoor basketball scenarios. MAEM achieves MPJPE/PA-MPJPE scores of 59.8/40.7 mm on SportCenter EPFL and 74.0/51.8 mm on Human-M3 Basketball, highlighting the effectiveness of dense mesh geometry for cross-view association without requiring target-domain training or fine-tuning.

</details>


### [70] [EVL-ECG: Efficient ECG Interpretation With Multi-Aspect Heterogeneous Knowledge Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29977)
*Dang Hong Nguyen,Nhi Ngoc-Yen Nguyen,Huy-Hieu Pham*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EVL-ECG 是一个专为跨架构心电信号诊断逻辑蒸馏设计的框架，通过多头交叉注意力对齐、最优传输视觉特征匹配和几何架构内关系匹配三种创新方法，在保持高保真心电图解读能力的同时显著降低计算需求，在基准测试上实现了最高 2.4% AUC 和 1.1% 临床准确率提升，并建立了适合资源受限临床环境的 2B 参数 ECG 基础模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真心电图解读日益依赖大规模基础模型，但这些模型在临床边缘计算环境中的部署受到极端计算需求的阻碍。知识蒸馏虽有潜力解决此问题，但传统方法在跨异构架构迁移知识时无法捕捉 ECG 信号复杂的时空依赖关系。

Method: EVL-ECG 提出三项 ECG 感知创新：(1) 多头交叉注意力对齐，协调架构差异以保留细粒度形态特征；(2) 基于最优传输的视觉特征匹配，在 token 表示不匹配的情况下维持跨导联全局结构关系；(3) 几何架构内关系匹配，蒸馏教师模型的潜在诊断推理能力。

Result: 在 ECG 基准测试上，EVL-ECG 相比现有基线方法提升了最高 2.4% AUC 和 1.1% 临床准确率，并成功建立了适用于资源受限临床环境的 2B 参数高效 ECG 基础模型。

Conclusion: EVL-ECG 通过专门设计的跨架构蒸馏方法，有效解决了大规模 ECG 模型在临床边缘部署的计算瓶颈，在保持高诊断精度的同时显著降低了模型规模，为资源受限环境下的智能心电诊断提供了可行方案。

Abstract: High-fidelity ECG interpretation is increasingly reliant on massive foundation models, yet their deployment in clinical edge-care remains hindered by extreme computational demands. While knowledge distillation (KD) is a promising solution, traditional methods fail to capture the complex spatio-temporal dependencies of ECG signals when transferring knowledge across heterogeneous architectures. In this paper, we propose EVL-ECG, a framework specifically designed for cross-architecture distillation of cardiac diagnostic logic. EVL-ECG introduces three ECG-aware innovations: (1) Multi-Head Cross-Attention Alignment, which harmonizes architectural discrepancies to preserve fine-grained morphological features; (2) Optimal Transport-based Visual Feature Matching, utilizing optimal transport to maintain global structural relationships across ECG leads despite mismatched token representations; and (3) Geometric Intra-Architecture Relation Matching, which distills the latent diagnostic reasoning of the teacher model. Evaluations across ECG benchmarks demonstrate that EVL-ECG yields improvements of up to 2.4% AUC and 1.1% clinical accuracy over existing baselines. Notably, EVL-ECG establishes an efficient 2B-parameter ECG foundation model, suitable for resource-constrained clinical environments.

</details>


### [71] [Genetically Aligned Patient Representations Improve Hematological Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29980)
*Muhammed Furkan Dasdelen,Fatih Ozlugedik,Ilaria Looser,Rao Muhammad Umer,Christian Pohlkamp,Carsten Marr*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GenBloom框架，通过两阶段训练（自监督视觉预训练+遗传对比对齐）将单细胞白细胞图像与染色体畸变和基因突变对齐，在血液肿瘤诊断任务中优于病理基础模型，并提供检索能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 血液细胞学诊断通常依赖单细胞视觉评估与细胞遗传学、分子遗传学数据的结合，但现有方法缺乏将单细胞图像与特定遗传变异（如核型异常、靶向基因突变）进行多模态对齐的框架，限制了诊断性能。

Method: 两阶段训练策略：(i) 在1500+患者队列上使用iBOT头进行自监督、纯视觉的Transformer聚合器预训练；(ii) 在急性髓系白血病患者上通过监督对比损失进行遗传对齐。将单细胞图像与染色体畸变（核型）和体细胞突变（靶向基因 panel）对齐。

Result: 遗传对齐后的患者编码器在血液肿瘤诊断任务中优于基于切片的病理基础模型；具备即用型的疾病和遗传变异检索能力；整合遗传数据提升了患者表示质量。

Conclusion: 该多模态框架与临床诊断工作流程一致，为未来血液学特异性多模态AI奠定了基础；代码和模型权重已开源。

Abstract: Multimodal alignment of histopathology encoders with transcriptomic and genomic data has been shown to significantly improve performance in downstream diagnostic tasks. Hematological cytology is unique in that visual single-cell evaluation is often paired with cytogenetics and molecular genetics for blood cancer diagnosis. In this study, we present a framework to align single white blood cell images with chromosomal aberrations (karyotype) and somatic mutations from targeted gene panels. Our training strategy follows a two-stage approach: (i) self-supervised, vision-only pretraining of a transformer aggregator using an iBOT head on a cohort of over 1500 patients, and (ii) genetic alignment via supervised contrastive loss on acute myeloid leukemia patients. Our genetically aligned patient encoder improves hematological diagnostic tasks, outperforming slide-level histopathology foundation models. Additionally, the model provides off-the-shelf retrieval capabilities for diseases and genetic alterations. Incorporating genetic data into patient encoders increases the quality of patient representations, providing a framework that aligns with clinical diagnostic workflows and paves the way for future multimodal hematology-specific AI. The code and model weights are available at https://github.com/marrlab/GenBloom.

</details>


### [72] [EarlyTom: Early Token Compression Completes Fast Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30010)
*Hesong Wang,Xin Jin,Lu Lu,Chenhaowen Li,Jian Chen,Qiang Liu,Huan Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出EarlyTom，一种无需训练的令牌压缩框架，通过在视觉编码器内部进行早期视觉令牌压缩，显著减少视频大语言模型的首令牌时间和计算开销，同时保持准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Video-LLMs的视觉令牌压缩方法大多仅在预填充后期进行，未优化视觉编码器的效率，而视觉编码过程占据了首令牌时间（TTFT）的很大比例，存在效率提升空间。

Method: 提出EarlyTom框架，在视觉编码器内部进行早期视觉令牌压缩（无需额外训练），并引入解耦的空间令牌选择策略以提升压缩效果。

Result: 在LLaVA-OneVision-7B模型上，EarlyTom在单个NVIDIA A100 GPU上实现了最高2.65倍的TTFT降低和61%的FLOPs减少，同时保持与全令牌基线相当的准确率。

Conclusion: EarlyTom通过早期视觉令牌压缩显著提升了Video-LLMs的实际部署效率，增强了其在真实生产场景中的实用性。

Abstract: Video large language models (Video-LLMs) have demonstrated strong capabilities in video understanding tasks. However, their practical deployment is still hindered by the inefficiency introduced by processing massive amounts of visual tokens. Although recent approaches achieve extremely low token retention ratios while maintaining accuracy comparable to full-token baselines, most of them perform compression only at the late stage of prefilling, leaving the efficiency of the vision encoder unoptimized. In this paper, we first show that vision encoding contributes a large portion to the time-to-first-token (TTFT). Therefore, instead of compressing visual tokens only after the vision encoder, performing compression inside the encoder still leaves substantial room for exploration. Based on this insight, we propose EarlyTom, a training-free token compression framework that performs early-stage visual token compression inside the vision encoder, enabling significantly better TTFT reduction and higher throughput. In addition, we introduce a decoupled spatial token selection strategy that improves the overall compression effectiveness. EarlyTom reduces TTFT by up to 2.65x and FLOPs by up to 61% on a single NVIDIA A100 GPU for the LLaVA-OneVision-7B model, while maintaining accuracy comparable to the full-token baseline. These improvements substantially enhance the practicality of deploying Video-LLMs in real-world production scenarios.

</details>


### [73] [VisualThink-VLA: Visual Intermediate Reasoning for Effective and Low-Latency Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30011)
*Mingjian Gao,Wenqiao Zhang,Yuqian Yuan,Yang Dai,Binhe Yu,Zheqi Lv,Haoyu Zheng,Jiaqi Zhu,Zhiqi Ge,Zixuan Wan,Siliang Tang,Yueting Zhuang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VISUALTHINK-VLA提出了一种视觉中间推理框架，通过紧凑的视觉证据接口和选择性路由机制，在保持高精度的同时将推理增强型基线的多秒延迟降低到亚秒级（如BridgeData V2上从8.377秒降至0.367秒，加速22.8倍）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA策略引入的文本链式思维（chain-of-thought）存在两个问题：一是弱文本信息或无关信息干扰动作预测，二是自回归文本解码带来过大延迟，无法满足实时闭环控制需求。

Method: 1) 提出紧凑视觉证据接口（visual-evidence interface），在保留空间精度的同时避免解码开销；2) 设计选择性路由机制（selective routing）学习视觉证据token，实现低延迟推理并保持高容量专业化；3) 构建VisualEvidence-Kit资源，包含VisualEvidence-Agent生成的754.7k指令集VisualEvidence-Set，用于路由监督和反事实忠实性测试。

Result: 在多项基准和真实机器人评估中，VISUALTHINK-VLA在大多数基准上取得了最高成功率，同时将延迟从多秒降至亚秒级。BridgeData V2上步骤延迟从8.377秒（ECoT）降至0.367秒，实现22.8倍加速。

Conclusion: 视觉中间推理相比文本链式思维更适合具身控制任务，VISUALTHINK-VLA通过视觉证据引导动作预测，兼顾了精度与实时性。

Abstract: Recent work has begun to equip vision-language-action (VLA) policies with explicit intermediate reasoning. In embodied control, however, textual chain-of-thought is a poor fit: irrelevant or weakly textual information can interfere with action prediction, while autoregressive text decoding adds too much latency for real-time closed-loop execution. We present VISUALTHINK-VLA, a visual intermediate-reasoning framework for accurate, low-latency VLA policies. Our bootstrapping philosophy is to guide action with effective visual thinking: VISUALTHINK-VLA bootstraps action prediction through a compact visual-evidence interface that preserves spatial precision while avoiding decoding overhead. Besides, to further improve performance and efficiency, VISUALTHINK-VLA adopts a tailored selective routing mechanism to learn the visual evidence tokens, enabling low-latency inference while preserving high-capacity specialization. We also introduce VisualEvidence-Kit, a supervision-and-audit resource centered on a VisualEvidence-Agent that constructs a 754.7k VLA instructions VisualEvidence-Set for route supervision and counterfactual faithfulness tests. Across multiple benchmarks and real-robot evaluation, VISUALTHINK-VLA achieves the highest success rate on most benchmarks while reducing the multi-second latency of reasoning-augmented baselines to the sub-second regime. For example, on BridgeData V2, it reduces step latency from 8.377,s with ECoT to 0.367,s, achieving a 22.8 times speedup.

</details>


### [74] [DocRetriever: A Plug-and-Play Framework for Multimodal Document Retrieval with Comprehensive Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30027)
*Ruofan Hu,Menghui Zhu,Jieming Zhu,Bo Chen,Shengyang Xu,Minjie Hong,Xiaoda Yang,Sashuai Zhou,Li Tang,Tao Jin,Zhou Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DocRetriever是一个即插即用的多模态文档检索框架，通过布局感知的稀疏嵌入技术实现无需OCR的混合编码，结合推理增强的少样本重排序器，并在新构建的MultiDocR基准上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖粗粒度的稠密嵌入和监督重排序器，存在显式语义模糊、泛化瓶颈以及缺乏多维度评估基准的问题。

Method: 提出DocRetriever框架：(1) 布局感知的稀疏嵌入技术实现混合编码，无需OCR；(2) 推理增强的少样本重排序器；(3) 构建MultiDocR多维度评估基准。

Result: 在多个基准上验证了DocRetriever优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: DocRetriever通过布局感知的稀疏嵌入和推理增强重排序，有效解决了多模态文档检索中的语义模糊和泛化问题。

Abstract: Multimodal documents contain diverse elements, such as tables, figures, and layouts, which can complicate retrieval tasks.
